# Skills · S · part 223

797 entries, socratic-tutor to software-architect. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **socratic-tutor**: Creates &lt;80-line blind-writing coding challenges (# TODO(human) + deterministic asserts and numerical gradient checks) in concepts/ without giving away full solutions. Use ONLY when the user explicitly asks to practice/blind-write ("练一下", "盲写", "出个练习") or works directly inside concepts/; for general course learning, derivations, code walkthroughs, and HTML notes, use lab-teach instead. - **socratic_questioning**: 苏格拉底式提问：当用户困惑模糊、嘴上问的和心里想的不一致时，通过最多 6 个追问 帮用户找到真正值得回答的问题。每次只问一个，根据回答决定下一问。 触发词：苏格拉底提问、苏格拉底式问诊、澄清困惑、找到真正的问题、问清问题、 我到底想问什么、socratic questioning。 本 skill 是 daily/ 思维工具集的一员，可与其他思维工具组合使用， 详见 daily/README.md 组合哲学。 ([Stargazed-Dreamer/Parcae](https://github.com/Stargazed-Dreamer/Parcae/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/daily/socratic_questioning) · ★4 · 2 copies)

- **socratica**: Fact-check answers, drafts, and documents against supplied or retrieved evidence, reporting supported, contradicted, or unverifiable claims with verbatim source quotes. Splits the text into atomic factual claims and gives each one a verdict of supported, contradicted, or unverifiable, backed by verbatim quotes from the sources, with a correction when the evidence states the right fact. Use when the user asks to fact-check, verify claims, detect hallucinations, or check text against sources or documents. ([Sathvik150/socratica](https://github.com/Sathvik150/socratica/tree/HEAD/skill/socratica) · ★3)

- **socratico**: Método socrático de aprendizaje jurídico. Plantea preguntas al estudiante, escucha su respuesta, empuja con contraargumentos e hipotéticos. NUNCA da la respuesta directamente. Se adapta a la materia (civil, penal, administrativo, etc.) según la configuración del perfil. Usar cuando el usuario dice "método socrático", "pregúntame", "hazme pensar", "debate jurídico", o quiere practicar el razonamiento legal a través de preguntas. - **socratico**: Sessão socrática sobre um capítulo de um livro do `study/` do vault: o Claude é tutor, monta casos fictícios, faz uma pergunta por vez, cobra as definições dos termos-chave, intercala termos de outros capítulos pelo placar do livro e corrige contra o livro. No "fechar", propõe cards do repeater a partir dos erros e hesitações do dono, mais o placar atualizado, e grava só depois da aprovação. Use quando o dono quiser estudar, revisar ou ser testado num capítulo de um livro de `study/` ("socrático do cap. 6", "me testa no capítulo 4"). Argumentos: a pasta do livro e o número do capítulo. Use especially for qualitative work that risks staying at the level of description. - **socs-fit-positioning**: Use to judge whether a manuscript fits 《社会学研究》 (Sociological Studies) — distinguishing a genuine sociological problematic from an economics-style policy-evaluation paper, and re-routing the latter to economics or cross-disciplinary journals. Use first, before drafting, when discipline fit is uncertain. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Sociological-Studies-Skills/skills/socs-fit-positioning) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **socs-mechanism-social-process**: Use to translate coefficients or fieldwork materials into a social process for 《社会学研究》 (Sociological Studies) — specifying who, via what mechanism, under what structural conditions, forms what social outcome or structure. Use when results stop at significance or at a vivid story without an explicit mechanism. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Sociological-Studies-Skills/skills/socs-mechanism-social-process) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **socs-problematic**: Use to build a sociological problem consciousness (问题意识) for 《社会学研究》 (Sociological Studies) — turning a variable relationship or a pile of fieldwork into a question about how a social mechanism, structure, or process works. Use when the manuscript still reads as "X affects Y" without a sociological stake. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Sociological-Studies-Skills/skills/socs-problematic) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **socs-qualitative**: Use for the qualitative path in 《社会学研究》 (Sociological Studies) — fieldwork ethnography, in-depth interviews, the extended case method, or grounded theory. Ensures material richness, coding transparency, reflexivity, and trustworthiness, and pushes from story-retelling toward concept building. Use when the draft is a narrative without analytic traction. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Sociological-Studies-Skills/skills/socs-qualitative) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **socs-quantitative**: Use for the quantitative path in 《社会学研究》 (Sociological Studies) — analyzing survey data (CGSS/CFPS/CLDS) with regression / Logit / multilevel / event-history (survival) / sequence analysis, and interpreting results as a sociological mechanism rather than entering an econometric identification arms race. Use when the draft only reports coefficients. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Sociological-Studies-Skills/skills/socs-quantitative) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **socs-rebuttal**: Use to draft the response letter for an R&R at 《社会学研究》 (Sociological Studies) — addressing reviewer concerns about sociological problematic, theory dialogue, mechanism/social process, qualitative trustworthiness, or quantitative interpretation, point by point, with respect and evidence. Use only after the manuscript itself has been revised. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Sociological-Studies-Skills/skills/socs-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **socs-style**: Use when polishing the language of a 《社会学研究》 (Sociological Studies) manuscript — removing both policy-report tone (加强/完善/推进) and pure-technique tone (method-flexing), and restoring a scholarly sociological register with paragraph-leading topic sentences. Use after the problematic and mechanism are settled. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Sociological-Studies-Skills/skills/socs-style) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **socs-submission**: Use for the pre-submission preflight to 《社会学研究》 (Sociological Studies) — checking in-text author-date citation (作者, 年: 页) plus end references (GB/T 7714, A–Z, 中外文分列), explanatory footnotes, abstract/keyword limits, the official online system (the journal's only channel), anonymity, and document hygiene. Verify every rule against the journal's current 投稿指南. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Sociological-Studies-Skills/skills/socs-submission) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **socs-theory-dialogue**: Use to build genuine dialogue with classic and contemporary sociological theory for 《社会学研究》 (Sociological Studies) — entering a theoretical lineage (stratification, networks, institutions, culture, action) rather than piling up recent empirical citations. Use when the literature review is a variable list, not a conversation. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Sociological-Studies-Skills/skills/socs-theory-dialogue) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **socs-workflow**: Use when deciding which socs-* sub-skill to invoke next, or when sequencing manuscript work from problematic framing through rebuttal for 《社会学研究》 (Sociological Studies). Routes — does not replace — the specialized skills, and re-routes off-discipline drafts to other journal packs. - **socsci-paper-orchestrator**: 사회과학 학술논문을 에이전트 팀으로 작성하는 오케스트레이터. 연구주제·메모·데이터·선행연구를 받아 연구설계→설계검토 게이트→근거조사·데이터분석→집필→다관점 검수→Word(.docx)·Markdown 변환까지 6인 팀(paper-designer, paper-investigator, paper-analyst, paper-writer, paper-reviewer, paper-finalizer)을 조율한다. - **soctheory-argument-development**: Use when stress-testing the reasoning of a Sociological Theory (ST) manuscript — turning stated propositions into a valid, warranted argument that survives rival theories and counter-cases. Develops and audits the argument; it does NOT define the concepts (soctheory-theory-construction) or bound the theory's domain (soctheory-boundary-conditions). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Sociological-Theory-Skills/skills/soctheory-argument-development) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **soctheory-boundary-conditions**: Use when specifying the scope, domain, and limits of a Sociological Theory (ST) manuscript's theory — stating where it holds, where it does not, and what it does NOT claim. Bounds the theory as a contribution; it does NOT build the concepts (soctheory-theory-construction) or audit the reasoning (soctheory-argument-development). Enforces fail-closed 6-stage verification (Atomic Scope -&gt; Zero-Mock Scan -&gt; Typecheck -&gt; Unit/Integration Tests -&gt; Contract Parity -&gt; Codex Review) to guarantee zero bugs and zero hallucinated regressions before goal completion or release. Triggers: zero bug, no bugs, quality guarantee, defect prevention, pre-ship gate, quality gate, zero-mock gate. Collaboration phases PLAN → EXECUTE → REVIEW → SHIP. ([Sodality-Company-Limited/live-pilot-os](https://github.com/Sodality-Company-Limited/live-pilot-os/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/soda-zero-bug-quality-guarantee))
- **sodactl**: Sodashitsu（コマンド soda）の pane の中から、sodactl で workspace・tab・pane・コーディングエージェントを調べて操作する。利用者が soda か sodactl を明示したとき、または soda の pane を使って隣の pane でコマンドを走らせる・別のエージェントに作業を頼むよう頼まれたときだけ使う。バックグラウンドの端末や並行作業が役立ちそうだというだけでは使わない。SODA_PANE_ID が要る。 ([asaomaro/sodashitsu](https://github.com/asaomaro/sodashitsu/tree/HEAD/packages/cli/skills/sodactl))
- **sodam-deploy**: 소담 봇(sodam_bot) 코드를 서버에 올리는 전체 흐름 — 전체 테스트 → 커밋·푸시(claude/button-panels) → PR 만들고 main 에 합치기 → 서버 자동 갱신(10분 타이머, 서버에서 테스트 약 20분)을 원격 점검 창구로 실제 버전까지 확인 → 한국어로 보고. 사용자가 "배포", "올려", "서버 반영됐어?", "배포됐어?", "PR 합쳐", "서버 갱신 실패" 메시지(🛠 서버 갱신 실패 tests_failed …)를 보여 줄 때, 또는 기능·버그 수정이 끝나 서버에 반영해야 할 때 꼭 이 스킬을 쓴다. ([moneyally/sodam_bot](https://github.com/moneyally/sodam_bot/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sodam-deploy))
- **sodam-diag**: 소담 봇 운영 서버를 클로드 작업 환경에서 읽기 전용으로 들여다보는 방법 — 원격 점검 창구(tools/diag.py — health·rooms·settings·messages·agent_runs·voice·modlog·counters·user·tables·logs)와 텔레그램 Bot API 읽기(getChat·getChatMember)로 "실측". 사용자가 "db 봐봐", "서버 로그", "왜 안 먹혀?", "이 사람 누구야/어느 방에 있어", "권한 왜 안 돼", "설정 확인", "실측으로 체크", 방 이름(벳블리·백악관·뉴월드 등)을 대며 무슨 일이 있었는지 물을 때, 또는 버그 원인을 추측이 아니라 운영 데이터로 확인해야 할 때 꼭 쓴다. ([moneyally/sodam_bot](https://github.com/moneyally/sodam_bot/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sodam-diag))
- **sodam-guide-docs**: 소담 공식 안내서(sodam/guide/*.md — 소담이 AI 도구 sodam_guide 로 읽고 방에서 답하는 지식 문서)를 쓰거나 고치는 규칙. 소담 기능·가격·결제·버튼·명령이 바뀌었을 때, 사용자가 "소담이가 ○○를 모르던데", "가격 안내", "문서 만들어", "안내서", "지식그래프", "소담이 알게 해줘" 라고 할 때, 새 기능을 추가해서 소담이 그 기능을 설명할 수 있어야 할 때 꼭 이 스킬을 쓴다. ([moneyally/sodam_bot](https://github.com/moneyally/sodam_bot/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sodam-guide-docs))
- **sodam-harness-beginner-tone**: [통합됨] beginner-tone 스킬로 통합됐습니다. 이 스킬 대신 beginner-tone을 사용하세요. ([sodam-ai/SoDam-Harness-Eng](https://github.com/sodam-ai/SoDam-Harness-Eng/tree/HEAD/skills/sodam-harness-beginner-tone) · ★1)

- **sodam-loop-checkup**: 정기점검(report-only) — 저장해둔 목표를 다시 검사만 하고 절대 고치지 않습니다. "점검해줘"·"정기점검"·`/sodam-loop:checkup` 등에서 호출. 무인 자동의 첫 단계(아직 반자동). ([sodam-ai/SoDam-Loop-Eng](https://github.com/sodam-ai/SoDam-Loop-Eng/tree/HEAD/skills/sodam-loop-checkup) · ★2)

- **sodam-loop-install**: SoDamLoop 첫 설치 안내. 설치 직후 또는 "설치 확인"·"처음 어떻게 써" 등에서 호출. 무엇이고 어떻게 시작하는지 왕초보에게 안내한다. ([sodam-ai/SoDam-Loop-Eng](https://github.com/sodam-ai/SoDam-Loop-Eng/tree/HEAD/skills/sodam-loop-install) · ★2)
- **sodam-loop-repair**: sodam-loop:start가 상태를 running으로 만든 뒤 도는 반복 루프. 만들기→검사→수정→재검사를 상한·시간·no-op·취소·streak 안에서 반복한다. sodam-loop:start 없이 단독 호출 금지. ([sodam-ai/SoDam-Loop-Eng](https://github.com/sodam-ai/SoDam-Loop-Eng/tree/HEAD/skills/sodam-loop-repair) · ★2)
- **sodam-loop-start**: SoDamLoop의 유일한 안전 시작 진입점. 사용자가 "이거 될 때까지 고쳐줘"(repair) 또는 "이 목표를 이룰 때까지 해줘"·"목표를 이뤄줘"·"달성해줘"·"목표달성"(goal) 류의 반복 작업을 요청하면, 클로드 코드 자체의 범용 /loop 기능이나 다른 플러그인의 반복 기능이 아니라 항상 이 스킬로 시작한다 — 그래야만 백업·위험명령확인·자기보호 등 SoDamLoop 안전장치가 켜진다. 목표 다듬기·자동 백업·안내·확인을 거친 뒤 repair 또는 goal 루프를 켠다. ([sodam-ai/SoDam-Loop-Eng](https://github.com/sodam-ai/SoDam-Loop-Eng/tree/HEAD/skills/sodam-loop-start) · ★2)
